# Commands · W · part 6

1,348 entries, wrap to write-sourcing-search-strings. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **wrap**: End a coding session - write the session's queued memories to Hindsight once (a script, no model), and only if you ask for them, run ONE review over everything the session built (Codex, adversarial tests, a Fable second opinion). Run it once after many features, never per feature. Usage - /feature-ladder:wrap [--review] [--adversarial] [--second-opinion] [--base &lt;ref&gt;] ([jakemctigue/feature-ladder](https://github.com/jakemctigue/feature-ladder/blob/HEAD/plugins/feature-ladder/commands/wrap.md))

- **wrapup**: &gt; **RULE 0 — NO PROSE.** State what is true; delete the rest. No justification, no precedent, no archaeology — that is what `git log` is for. Every sentence is a claim that can be false, so fewer sentences means fewer defects. If a fact is derivable, ship the command, not the paragraph.
